## 2026-09-06 20:21:29.072Z click
- element: {"tag":"p","role":null,"ariaLabel":null,"name":null,"type":null,"id":null,"placeholder":null,"label":null,"value":null,"valueLength":0,"text":"Delta Sigma Pi Business Fraternity was founded at New York University in 1907 to bring business students together around scholarship, professional development, and fellowship. Today, more than 300 chapters and 300,000 initiated members form one of the largest professional networks in the world of business. The Alpha Zeta Chapter of Delta Sigma Pi has been part of the University of Tennessee since 1924. Today, our chapter is made up of students from across the Haslam College of Business who share an interest in building their careers, serving the Knoxville community, and making the most of their time at UT. What starts as a professional organization quickly becomes much more. Between chapter meetings, professional events, service projects, formals, and everything in between, DSP gives our brothers a place to learn from each other and form genuine friendships."}

- element: {"tag":"section","role":null,"ariaLabel":null,"name":null,"type":null,"id":null,"placeholder":null,"label":null,"value":null,"valueLength":0,"text":"Our StoryFounded in 1907. At Rocky Top since 1924.Delta Sigma Pi Business Fraternity was founded at New York University in 1907 to bring business students together around scholarship, professional development, and fellowship. Today, more than 300 chapters and 300,000 initiated members form one of the largest professional networks in the world of business.The Alpha Zeta Chapter of Delta Sigma Pi has been part of the University of Tennessee since 1924. Today, our chapter is made up of students from across the Haslam College of Business who share an interest in building their careers, serving the Knoxville community, and making the most of their time at UT. What starts as a professional organization quickly becomes much more. Between chapter meetings, professional events, service projects, formals, and everything in between, DSP gives our brothers a place to learn from each other and form genuine friendships.The Alpha Zeta Chapter carries that legacy at the University of Tennessee, Knoxville. Rooted in the Haslam College of Business, our chapter pairs career-building programming with the kind of community that makes a campus of 30,000 feel like home.Meet the Brothers Previous slideNext slidePrevious slideNext slide"}

- element: {"tag":"section","role":null,"ariaLabel":null,"name":null,"type":null,"id":null,"placeholder":null,"label":null,"value":null,"valueLength":0,"text":"A Message from Our President, Finley:\"Welcome to the Alpha Zeta Chapter of Delta Sigma Pi!\n\nWhen I first walked through the doors as a potential recruit four semesters ago, I had no idea how much this fraternity would shape my college experience. Now, stepping into my fifth semester with Alpha Zeta, and my term as Chapter President, I am deeply honored to lead an organization that has given me so much. Delta Sigma Pi has provided me with a strong professional foundation, mentors who have become my best friends, and a community that pushes me to grow every day.\n\nOur fraternity is built on the pillars of Professionalism, Brotherhood, Community Service, and Scholarship. But what truly defines the Alpha Zeta Chapter is our people.\n\nWe are a diverse group of driven students who push each other toward excellence, whether that means prepping for interviews together, giving back through local service projects, or simply supporting one another through life's many obstacles. The skills we build here pave the way for successful careers, but the bonds we create last a lifetime.\n\nWhether you are a student exploring recruitment, an alumnus looking to reconnect, or a corporate partner interested in working with our driven members, I encourage you to look around and learn more about our chapter.\n\nThank you for visiting, and please feel free to reach out if you'd like to learn more about what Delta Sigma Pi means to us.\n\nWarmly,\nFinley Roland\""}

## 2026-09-08 21:01:07.046Z click
- element: {"tag":"div","role":null,"ariaLabel":null,"name":null,"type":null,"id":null,"placeholder":null,"label":null,"value":null,"valueLength":0,"text":"Our StoryFounded in 1907. At Rocky Top since 1924.Delta Sigma Pi Business Fraternity was founded at New York University in 1907 to bring business students together around scholarship, professional development, and fellowship. Today, more than 300 chapters and 300,000 initiated members form one of the largest professional networks in the world of business.The Alpha Zeta Chapter of Delta Sigma Pi has been part of the University of Tennessee since 1924. Today, our chapter is made up of students from across the Haslam College of Business who share an interest in building their careers, serving the Knoxville community, and making the most of their time at UT. What starts as a professional organization quickly becomes much more. Between biweekly chapter meetings, professional events, service projects, formals, and everything in between, DSP gives our brothers a place to learn from each other and form genuine friendships.The Alpha Zeta Chapter carries that legacy at the University of Tennessee, Knoxville. Rooted in the Haslam College of Business, our chapter pairs career-building programming with the kind of community that makes a campus of 30,000 feel like home.Meet the Brothers A Look Inside Our BrotherhoodOur storyPrevious slideNext slide"}

## 2026-09-08 21:18:25.324Z click
- element: {"tag":"blockquote","role":null,"ariaLabel":null,"name":null,"type":null,"id":null,"placeholder":null,"label":null,"value":null,"valueLength":0,"text":"Welcome to the Alpha Zeta Chapter of Delta Sigma Pi!\n\nWhen I first walked through the doors as a potential recruit four semesters ago, I had no idea how much this fraternity would shape my college experience. Now, stepping into my fifth semester with Alpha Zeta, and my term as Chapter President, I am deeply honored to lead an organization that has given me so much. Delta Sigma Pi has provided me with a strong professional foundation, mentors who have become my best friends, and a community that pushes me to grow every day.\n\nOur fraternity is built on the pillars of Professionalism, Brotherhood, Community Service, and Scholarship. But what truly defines the Alpha Zeta Chapter is our people.\n\nWe are a diverse group of driven students who push each other toward excellence, whether that means prepping for interviews together, giving back through local service projects, or simply supporting one another through life's many obstacles. The skills we build here pave the way for successful careers, but the bonds we create last a lifetime.\n\nWhether you are a student exploring recruitment, an alumnus looking to reconnect, or a corporate partner interested in working with our driven members, I encourage you to look around and learn more about our chapter.\n\nThank you for visiting, and please feel free to reach out if you'd like to learn more about what Delta Sigma Pi means to us.\n\nWarmly,\nFinley Roland"}

## 2026-09-08 21:18:35.091Z click

Delta Sigma Pi has provided me with a strong professional foundation, mentors who have become my best friends, and a community that pushes me to grow every day.\n\nOur fraternity is built on the pillars of Professionalism, Brotherhood, Community Service, and Scholarship. But what truly defines the Alpha Zeta Chapter is our people.\n\nWe are a diverse group of driven students who push each other toward excellence, whether that means prepping for interviews together, giving back through local service projects, or simply supporting one another through life's many obstacles. The skills we build here pave the way for successful careers, but the bonds we create last a lifetime.\n\nWhether you are a student exploring recruitment, an alumnus looking to reconnect, or a corporate partner interested in working with our driven members, I encourage you to look around and learn more about our chapter.\n\nThank you for visiting, and please feel free to reach out if you'd like to learn more about what Delta Sigma Pi means to us.\n\nWarmly,\nFinley Roland"}
